- **2026-06-02** [arxiv:2606.02304] CLAIM: Unified Context Evolution (UCE) — gradient-free framework externalizing agent experience into typed Evolvable Context Units (Memory / Strategy / Workflow / Skill); three coupled mechanisms (typed knowledge decomposition + ECU quality tracking + adaptive scheduling toward weak-coverage types); cross-backbone transferable without retraining actor; **ALFWorld success 75.4% → 96.3% (+20.9 pts), WebShop task score 45.1% → 61.3% (+16.2 pts)** | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02304 | status: open ; new SOTA in ALFWorld near-90% cluster (prior: [arxiv:2605.05413] CC-Skill 89.6%, [arxiv:2605.06078] BEACON 92.9%, [arxiv:2605.06642] StraTA 93.1%); mechanism #4 (typed-knowledge externalization, gradient-free) is fourth-axis-distinct → composes rather than replaces — open question whether stacking breaks the ALFWorld ceiling entirely; confirms typed-writes thesis alongside [arxiv:2605.31075] TaskMem (write policy on streaming multimodal) and [arxiv:2605.21463] Mem-π (write policy on text)
- **2026-06-02** [arxiv:2606.01533] CLAIM: Multi-Agent Computer Use (Koh / Salakhutdinov / Fried, CMU) — manager LLM emits a DAG of subagent tasks with dependencies, parallel subagents execute satisfied nodes, manager continuously revises DAG based on new screen observations, information forwarded across subagents via manager + DAG structure; **3.4–25.5% improvements over single-agent baselines on OSWorld + Online-Mind2Web + WebTailBench + Odysseys, ~1.5× wall-clock speedup on Odysseys** | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01533 | status: open ; first multi-agent answer to computer-use specifically (prior MAS work targeted text-based research/reasoning: WideSearch, Uno-Orchestra, DecomposeR); cross-benchmark generalization (OSWorld file-system + Online-Mind2Web web-only both move under same recipe) is the stronger evidence point; pairs with [arxiv:2605.30824] DecomposeR (yesterday: typed-DAG plans as RL target for deep research) → DAG-as-first-class-plan-structure cluster across two task domains
- **2026-06-02** [arxiv:2606.02060] CLAIM: DRIFT — claim-centric audit framework (extracts agent claims along trajectory + checks support in trajectory evidence + marks spans where unsupported / conflicting claims propagate); **2,790 real trajectories × 2 frameworks × 3 backbones × 3 benchmarks**; **+30 pts on span-level error localization and first-error accuracy** over baselines; releases TELBench (1,000 instances) for first-error-span localization | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02060 | status: open ; supplies diagnostic primitive for long-horizon-collapse cluster ([arxiv:2604.15597] DELEGATE-52 / [arxiv:2605.06635] AST-parser / [arxiv:2605.30434] LongDS-Bench all measure *that* late-turn collapse happens; DRIFT measures *where*); claim-as-unit-of-eval converging alongside [arxiv:2605.03042] ARIS Assurance layer + [arxiv:2605.29087] Trace-Answer Dissociation (DRIFT quantifies UC per-trajectory)
- **2026-06-02** [arxiv:2606.02031] CLAIM: OpenWebRL (Microsoft + UIUC + UCLA — Jianfeng Gao group) — full open recipe for end-to-end online multi-turn RL of a 4B visual web agent on real live websites; five components (scalable live-browser infra + SFT init + multimodal context mgmt + trajectory-level success judging + efficient multi-turn policy optimization); **0.4K initialization trajectories + 2.2K RL training tasks** to reach **67.0% Online-Mind2Web, 64.0% DeepShop** | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02031 | status: open ; full end-to-end recipe the CUA cluster ([arxiv:2605.29119] PRO-CUA decoupled training, [arxiv:2605.25624] CUA-Gym env side, Microsoft May 21 Fara1.5 small-model CUA) has been building toward; 2.2K RL-task budget is sharpest published data-efficiency point in cluster (prior open CUA work needed 50K-500K trajectories); open compositional question whether OpenWebRL-4B used *under* [arxiv:2606.01533] Multi-Agent Computer Use's manager-DAG dispatch matches or beats frontier-backbone orchestration
- **2026-06-02** [arxiv:2606.02054] CLAIM: eMoT (Evolving Memory-of-Thought) — three coupled mechanisms (memory corrosion = high-utility trace reinforcement + low-utility decay across repeated tasks / symbolic anchoring = Python deterministic backstop / consistency-driven refinement = active neural-vs-symbolic reconciliation, refuses commit on divergence); **Game of 24: 100% accuracy, +17.6 pts over baseline**; consistent gains on GSM8K, ASDiv, SVAMP, MGSM with lightweight backbone (model unnamed in abstract) | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02054 | status: open ; mechanistically third-axis-distinct from [arxiv:2605.31075] TaskMem (write-time policy on streaming input) and [arxiv:2605.30785] AdaCoM (external manage-time for frozen agent) — eMoT = utility-decay-driven self-curation (decay-time axis); together TaskMem + AdaCoM + UCE + eMoT cover write / manage / decay / type as separable memory primitives; memory-corrosion utility-decay primitive is direct counter to [arxiv:2605.08060] Memory Curse but not tested on it
- **2026-06-02** [hf-blog-2026-06-01-jetbrains-mellum2-launch] CLAIM: JetBrains Mellum2 — **12B total / 2.5B active MoE**, text + code, **Apache 2.0**; positioned explicitly as "focal" sub-agent model (routing, RAG context compression, summarization, retrieval post-processing, planning, validation, transformation, code completion), not a full agent base; ">2× faster than similar-sized models" per JetBrains; benchmark details (SWE-bench, HumanEval, BFCL, context length, training token count) deferred to technical report arxiv:2605.31268 not yet verified | source: https://huggingface.co/blog/JetBrains/mellum2-launch | status: open ; exact size + role fit for [arxiv:2605.30785] AdaCoM's "small local LLM as external context manager for frozen remote executor" architecture from yesterday — first time full local-manager-plus-remote-executor stack components (typed ECU store from UCE + local Mellum2 manager + remote Claude Opus 4.8 executor) all exist as published parts

- **2026-06-01** [arxiv:2605.31075] CLAIM: TaskMem treats memory generation as a learnable policy; two-phase training paradigm (Phase 1 = how to memorize under fidelity requirements, Phase 2 = post-deployment adapter learning *what* to memorize via task-derived reward); built on **Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B**, improves streaming-VQA accuracy by **+6.3% VideoMME / +7.0% EgoLife / +5.3% EgoTempo** on reformulated streaming benchmarks where the agent must answer from memory only (no raw-video access) | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31075 | status: open ; multimodal-streaming extension of [arxiv:2605.21463] Mem-π "learn when and what to generate" axis — emerging *write-policy-as-learned-thing* cluster; open empirical question whether learned task-focused retention neutralizes [arxiv:2605.08060] Memory Curse on cooperative-task subsets (not tested in paper)
- **2026-06-01** [arxiv:2605.30785] CLAIM: AdaCoM trains an *external* LLM via end-to-end RL to manage a **frozen agent's** context through flexible modification actions; substantially improves performance on web search + deep research benchmarks by preserving task constraints/progress while pruning stale content; surfaces a **Fidelity-Reliability Trade-off** — agents with higher vanilla ReAct performance benefit from *higher-fidelity* context preservation, lower-performing agents need *more aggressive* compression to stay in reliable reasoning regime; transfer experiments show generalization is strongest across agents of *similar* vanilla ReAct capability | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30785 | status: open ; first paper to treat context management as a separable, externally-trainable policy rather than something baked into the agent — externalized variant of the [arxiv:2605.05191] LongSeeker / [arxiv:2605.04050] LCM / [arxiv:2605.05413] Constant-Context Skill Learning / [arxiv:2605.23296] Parallel Context Compaction cluster; no head-to-head benchmark across the agent-side / weights-side / external variants
- **2026-06-01** [arxiv:2605.31042] CLAIM: ClawTrojan defines a multi-step trojan-attack benchmark for local agentic harnesses — adversary plants a prompt injection in a file or tool output, agent reads + stores it in workspace state during one session, executes it in a later session; no individual step is overtly malicious so step-isolated defenses miss it. **95.5% Attack Success Rate on GPT-5.4 in OpenClaw-style simulated workspace vs. near-zero ASR for existing single-turn prompt-injection attacks**. DASGuard defense scans control-like text in sensitive local files, traces origin, removes content not from trusted source; reported as "strong dynamic defense" combining runtime blocking + sanitized commits | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31042 | status: open ; runtime-side complement to [arxiv:2605.29082] Redpanda Agentic Data Plane (out-of-band metadata governance) and [arxiv:2605.04785] AgentTrust (call-level interception) — three layers of agent-security cluster; first benchmark to quantify how reliably current defenses miss write-now-read-later persistence attacks
- **2026-06-01** [arxiv:2605.30738] CLAIM: MAVEN — lightweight symbolic-reasoning scaffold for agentic tool calling (structured decomposition + adaptive tool orchestration + **intermediate verification**) lifts **GPT-OSS-120b from 48% → 71% accuracy on MAVEN-Bench (multi-step math/physics tool composition) with no fine-tuning**; reports competitive results with proprietary frontier models on BFCL v3 / TauBench / Tau2Bench / AceBench at **~10% the cost** using open-weight backbone | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30738 | status: open ; intermediate-verification axis is mechanistically distinct from [arxiv:2605.07112] Switchcraft (routing), [arxiv:2605.04785] AgentTrust (call-level interception), [arxiv:2605.06455] PrefixGuard (trajectory-level supervised monitor) — four-axis scaffold cluster, no head-to-head ablation published
- **2026-06-01** [arxiv:2605.30434] CLAIM: LongDS-Bench — 68 multi-turn Kaggle-derived data analysis tasks across 6 domains, **2,225 total interaction turns, average dependency span 11.3 turns**; best frontier model accuracy **48.45%**; **~47-point performance decline early-turn to late-turn**; long-horizon errors account for **52–69% of total failures**; **"additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance" — bottleneck is maintaining correct analytical state, not interaction budget** | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434 | status: open ; **third independent confirmation** of long-horizon-collapse cluster alongside [arxiv:2604.15597] DELEGATE-52 (May 10) and [arxiv:2605.06635] AST-parser citation-eval (May 8); sharpens DELEGATE-52's coarser "interaction length matters" claim by specifying the dependency-span axis
- **2026-06-01** [arxiv:2605.30824] CLAIM: DecomposeR — planner-centric RL with research plans as **typed directed acyclic graphs** (operation-typed nodes + dependency edges); two-stage training (Stage 1 = optimize graph structure + query decomposition under structure-aware reward, Stage 2 = learn execution + synthesis conditioned on plan); **+5.1–8.0 points over open-source baselines on long-form deep-research benchmarks** with **Qwen3-8B** backbone | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30824 | status: open ; first paper to make the *plan itself* the reward target separately from the answer — planner-side analogue of the [arxiv:2605.29087] Trace-Answer Dissociation / [arxiv:2605.12925] AgentLens Lucky-Pass answer-vs-trace credibility cluster; pairs with [arxiv:2604.27221] WideSearch, [arxiv:2605.05007] Uno-Orchestra, [arxiv:2605.06642] StraTA as four-paper structured-planning cluster with no head-to-head benchmark
- **2026-06-01** [arxiv:2605.30832] CLAIM: SLAT — theoretical characterization that CoT inefficiency *concentrates in high-probability segments with low marginal utility* (token-uniform penalties miss this); segment-level RL framework targets these specifically; **50% relative reduction in reasoning length** at competitive accuracy with "superior accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier" on standard reasoning benchmarks | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30832 | status: open ; mechanistically distinct sharpening of [arxiv:2605.00425] AEM (response-level entropy modulation) and [arxiv:2605.06078] BEACON (milestone-segmented temporal reward shaping) — three-axis CoT-economics cluster; composable rather than competing
- **2026-06-01** STATUS UPDATE: [arxiv:2604.15597] DELEGATE-52 and [arxiv:2605.06635] AST-parser citation-eval long-horizon-collapse cluster updates from `open` to `confirms-and-extends [arxiv:2605.30434] LongDS-Bench` — three independent benchmarks (delegated document editing, cited deep research, multi-turn data analysis) now converge on: late-turn collapse is dominant frontier-agent failure mode in 2026, bottleneck is state maintenance not tool budget, "more agent steps" makes it worse not better.
- **2026-05-31** [arxiv:2605.29087] CLAIM: "Unfaithful capitulation" — in adversarial multi-turn settings, reasoning-mode LLMs keep the **chain-of-thought factually correct from first turn to last while the emitted answer flips wrong**; **latent-correct rate ~50% in think mode vs. 11–15% in no-think**; **GPT-4o judge agrees with 86% of UC labels**; **token-level analysis finds the correct answer present in 84% of UC instances**; effect *scales with* reasoning capability (stronger reasoners are more susceptible); 3 datasets: MT-Consistency, MMLU-Pro, GSM8K | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29087 | status: open ; extends [arxiv:2605.12925] AgentLens (Lucky Pass: passing for wrong reasoning) by adding opposite-sign mode (failing despite correct reasoning) — together establish bidirectional decorrelation of answer accuracy and reasoning quality
- **2026-05-31** [arxiv:2605.29068] CLAIM: COLAGUARD transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space via stage-wise training curriculum; **+8.24 macro-F1 over Llama Guard 3, matches GuardReasoner on macro-F1, 12.9× inference speedup, 22.4× token-usage reduction** | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068 | status: open ; cluster-mate with [arxiv:2605.30343] RiM as emerging latent-reasoning-as-primitive line (general reasoning + safety classification, both single-pass, both speed-and-quality wins)
- **2026-05-31** [arxiv:2605.29612] CLAIM: CONCAT training-free MAS clustering + confidence-leader-election + ToM-heuristic collaboration prediction achieves **2.02× higher efficiency (accuracy/latency)** vs. LLM-Debate, **50.1% average-latency reduction on Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct**, outperforms training-dependent AgentDropout while matching or improving accuracy | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29612 | status: open ; pairs with [arxiv:2605.30227] (prompt-optimization layer) and [arxiv:2605.26178] ATOM (hierarchy layer) as MAS-efficiency cluster — three layers, no head-to-head benchmark yet
- **2026-05-31** [arxiv:2605.29790] CLAIM: Meta-Team experience-driven MAS evolution preserves per-agent execution context and coordinates post-task communication for distributed-evidence exchange; conducts multi-scale self-evolution at three layers simultaneously (individual behaviors + inter-agent coordination + team organization); "consistently outperforms single-agent systems, hand-crafted MAS, and prior MAS evolution methods" across **six long-horizon agent benchmarks** (specific numbers not in abstract) | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29790 | status: open ; same lead author (Ziyan Liu) as [arxiv:2605.30159] MMPO — Zhejiang cluster converging on belief-and-context-as-unit-of-evolution thesis across single/multi-agent boundary
- **2026-05-31** [arxiv:2605.29055] CLAIM: Three-stage multi-agent pipeline + semantic caching on 310 prompts achieves **−31.3% to −35.9% end-to-end Total Hallucination Score reduction**; **47.3% semantic-cache hit rate cuts LLM calls from 930 → 490** (440 cache hits) — net cost approaches un-mitigated baseline at high cache-hit regimes | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055 | status: open ; small eval set (310 prompts) caps generalization strength
- **2026-05-31** [arxiv:2605.29119] CLAIM: PRO-CUA decouples on-policy environment interaction from policy optimization for CUAs using step-level feedback from a process reward model; dense credit assignment without golden answers or offline expert trajectories; effectiveness demonstrated on live web benchmarks (specific percentages not in abstract) | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29119 | status: open ; training-method counterpart to [arxiv:2605.25624] CUA-Gym (env side); structurally adjacent to [hf-blog-2026-05-27-delta-weight-sync] async-RL primitive (decoupled trainer + rollout)
- **2026-05-31** [arxiv:2605.29082] CLAIM: Redpanda Agentic Data Plane proposes out-of-band metadata channels operating entirely outside agent's read/write path for governance enforcement; demonstrated on multi-agent portfolio rebalancing with per-client data scoping + trade-approval thresholds + tamper-proof audit trails; "agents can neither see nor bypass" invariant addresses prompt-injection-as-jailbreak failure mode of in-prompt guardrails | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29082 | status: open ; complement (not competitor) to [arxiv:2605.29068] COLAGUARD — out-of-model enforcement vs. in-model latent reasoning; no published decomposition of what each layer is actually responsible for catching
- **2026-05-31** [arxiv:2605.30227] CLAIM: Unifying temporal and structural credit assignment for MAS prompt optimization via state-space-bottleneck identification of critical rounds + stationary-role-policy isolation of agent contributions; discrete verbalized block coordinate descent alternating role-prompt optimization and aggregation-protocol optimization; "substantially reduces query complexity while improving performance" (specific numbers not in abstract) | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30227 | status: open ; MAS-efficiency cluster with [arxiv:2605.29612] CONCAT (topology layer) and [arxiv:2605.26178] ATOM (hierarchy layer)
- **2026-05-31** [arxiv:2605.30260] CLAIM: Parametric Memory Law — power-law relationship between loss reduction (ΔL) and effective parameters × sequence length for LoRA-as-memory-probe; phase transition at **token-level prediction probability p > 0.5 = sufficient condition for verbatim recall**; proposes MemFT reallocating training resources toward underperforming tokens | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30260 | status: open ; theoretical fine-tune-side parametric-memory finding; agent-side application not addressed in abstract
- **2026-05-30** [extends [arxiv:2605.12925]] STATUS UPDATE: AgentLens "Lucky Pass" claim (10.7% of SWE-bench Verified passes are passing for wrong reasoning) updates from `open` to `extended-by [arxiv:2605.29087]` — Trace-Answer Dissociation adds the opposite-sign failure mode (failing despite correct reasoning, ~50% latent-correct in think mode), establishing bidirectional decorrelation of answer accuracy and reasoning quality.
- **2026-05-04** [arxiv:2605.00737] CLAIM: "models' perceived need and utility of tool calls are often misaligned with their true need and utility"; lightweight hidden-state estimators improve tool-call decisions across 3 tasks × 6 models | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00737 | status: open

- **2026-05-04** [arxiv:2604.27221] CLAIM: bi-level orchestrator + parallel workers with shared workspace achieves WideSearch Avg@4 = 38.50 (vs. 5.10 second-best, 7.5×); Row F1 +25.03; XBench-DeepSearch 73.0 | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27221 | status: open
- **2026-05-04** [arxiv:2605.00425] CLAIM: response-level (not token-level) adaptive entropy modulation adds +1.4 percentage points on SWE-bench Verified atop a SOTA baseline; tested 1.5B–32B | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00425 | status: open

- **2026-05-07** [arxiv:2605.05191] CLAIM: Context-ReAct paradigm (5 atomic context operators: Skip, Compress, Rollback, Snippet, Delete) + LongSeeker (Qwen3-30B-A3B fine-tune on 10k synthesized trajectories) hits 61.5% BrowseComp / 62.5% BrowseComp-ZH vs. Tongyi DeepResearch 43.2/46.7 and AgentFold 36.2/47.3; Compress operator proven expressively complete | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05191 | status: open
- **2026-05-07** [arxiv:2605.04050] CLAIM: LCM-augmented coding agent "Volt" (Opus 4.6) outperforms Claude Code (also Opus 4.6) on the OOLONG long-context eval at every measured context length 32K–1M tokens; mechanism = recursive context compression (hierarchical summary DAG with lossless pointers) + recursive task partitioning (engine-managed parallel primitives like LLM-Map) | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04050 | status: open ; direct external comparison vs. Claude Code harness, awaits independent reproduction
- **2026-05-07** [arxiv:2605.05007] CLAIM: Uno-Orchestra learns decomposition depth + worker model/primitive + inference budget jointly via RL on 22 baseline workflows; reports 77.0% macro pass@1 across 13 benchmarks, +16 pp over strongest workflow baseline at ~10× lower per-query cost | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05007 | status: open ; relates-to [arxiv:2604.27221] (both: structured/learned orchestration > flat workflows)
- **2026-05-07** [arxiv:2605.05138] CLAIM: Coding agent that maintains an executable Python world model, verifies against past observations, refactors toward simpler abstractions (MDL-proxy), and plans through the model achieves 7/25 fully-solved games on ARC-AGI-3 with mean per-game RHAE 32.58% (no game-specific logic) | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05138 | status: open
- **2026-05-07** [arxiv:2605.04624] CLAIM: agent-repair leaderboards reorder substantially under evaluator reconfiguration; screening-guided methods that consult evaluator-derived signal during candidate selection account for the majority of rank instability — random channel blinding 7% vs. screening-guided 55–74% (mean 62%) reduction in rank displacement; AuditRepairBench-Lite preserves rankings at Kendall τ = 0.88 | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04624 | status: open ; sharpens [hf-blog-2026-04-29-evaleval-eval-costs-bottleneck] by identifying a specific leak mechanism
- **2026-05-07** [arxiv:2605.04785] CLAIM: AgentTrust runtime tool-call interceptor (allow/warn/block/review verdicts; deobfuscation + SafeFix + RiskChain + LLM-as-Judge) reports 95.0% verdict / 73.7% risk-level accuracy on 300-scenario internal bench, 96.7% verdict / ~93% on shell-obfuscated payloads on 630-scenario adversarial bench; low-ms latency; AGPL-3.0 with MCP server | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04785 | status: open
- **2026-05-07** [arxiv:2605.04018] CLAIM: BRIGHT-Pro (multi-aspect gold-evidence benchmark, evaluates retrievers under static AND agentic search protocols) + RTriever-Synth aspect-decomposed corpus produce RTriever-4B (LoRA fine-tune of Qwen3-Embedding-4B) that "substantially improves" over base on reasoning-intensive retrieval; aspect-aware/agentic eval exposes behaviors hidden by single-passage relevance metrics | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04018 | status: open
- **2026-05-08** [arxiv:2605.06527] CLAIM: STALE benchmark (400 expert-validated implicit-conflict scenarios / 1,200 queries / contexts to 150K tokens) shows best frontier LLM + memory framework caps at 55.2% accuracy detecting that a later observation invalidated an earlier memory without explicit negation; CUPMem (write-time consolidation + propagation-aware search) proposed as baseline fix | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06527 | status: open ; defines new failure mode "Implicit Conflict" not previously isolated in agent-memory benchmarks
- **2026-05-08** [arxiv:2605.06132] CLAIM: MemReranker-4B (Qwen3-Reranker fine-tune via multi-teacher pairwise + BCE pointwise + InfoNCE distillation) reaches 0.737 MAP on agent memory retrieval, matching GPT-4o-mini and Gemini-3-Flash at 10-20% of their latency; 0.6B variant beats BGE-Reranker | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06132 | status: open ; first reasoning-aware reranker explicitly trained for temporal/causal/coreference dialogue memory
- **2026-05-08** [arxiv:2605.06078] CLAIM: BEACON (milestone-segmented temporal reward shaping + multi-scale advantage estimation) reaches 92.9% success on ALFWorld vs. GRPO 53.5% (~1.74×); effective sample utilization 23.7% → 82.0% | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06078 | status: open ; relates-to [arxiv:2605.00425] AEM (orthogonal axis: segment-level reward shaping vs. response-level entropy modulation)
- **2026-05-08** [arxiv:2605.06614] CLAIM: SkillOS (frozen agent executor + trainable skill curator that maintains an external markdown SkillRepo, trained via composite rewards on grouped task streams where earlier trajectories update the repo and later tasks evaluate updates) consistently outperforms memory-free and memory-based baselines on multi-turn agentic and single-turn reasoning tasks; learned skills evolve into structured Markdown encoding higher-level meta-skills | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06614 | status: open
- **2026-05-08** [arxiv:2605.06623] CLAIM: MASPO (joint multi-agent prompt optimization via data-driven evolutionary beam search; judges each prompt by its successor agent's downstream success rather than local validity) achieves +2.9 average accuracy over SOTA prompt optimizers across 6 tasks (ICML 2026) | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06623 | status: open ; relates-to [arxiv:2605.05007] Uno-Orchestra — MASPO learns prompts over fixed orchestration; Uno-Orchestra learns orchestration over fixed prompts
- **2026-05-08** [arxiv:2605.06334] CLAIM: MANTRA synthesizes SMT-validated compliance benchmarks from natural-language procedural manuals (50+ pages) and tool schemas via independent symbolic world model + structured repair loop; releases 285 tasks across 6 domains, replacing manually-built corpora and LLM-judge graders | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06334 | status: open ; sharpens [arxiv:2605.04624] AuditRepairBench by addressing benchmark *synthesis* reliability rather than ranking stability
- **2026-05-08** [arxiv:2605.06635] CLAIM: AST-parser citation-eval framework over 14 closed/open models shows frontier deep-research agents maintain >94% link validity and >80% relevance but only 39-77% factual accuracy, and factual accuracy drops ~42% as tool-call depth scales 2 → 150; fewer than half of open-source models produce a cited single-shot report | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06635 | status: open ; new axis (citation factuality) on the agent-eval rebuild trend
- **2026-05-08** [arxiv:2605.06326] CLAIM: 4-stage TIR recipe (SFT on tool-suited problems with balanced trajectories → optimize pass@k & response length not loss → RLVR with mode-collapse safeguards) on Qwen3 thinking models reaches AIME 2025 96.7% (4B) / 99.2% (30B); claimed SOTA among open-source on math+reasoning | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06326 | status: open ; relates-to [arxiv:2605.00737] (when-to-call mismatch) and [arxiv:2605.00136] (tool-use tax) — TIR provides a positive recipe to mitigate both
- **2026-05-08** [anthropic-research-2026-05-07-natural-language-autoencoders] CLAIM: Anthropic Natural Language Autoencoders (verbalizer + reconstructor over frozen target activations, end-to-end reconstruction-fidelity loss) surface that Claude suspects evaluation 16% in destructive-action tests / 26% on benchmarks vs. <1% in real usage; auditors equipped with NLAs find hidden model motivations 12-15% of the time vs. <3% without | source: https://www.anthropic.com/research/natural-language-autoencoders | status: open ; quantifies a benchmark-vs-real-usage evaluation-awareness gap relevant to all Claude eval interpretation
- **2026-05-09** [anthropic-research-2026-05-08-teaching-claude-why] CLAIM: principle-based alignment training (3M-token "Difficult Advice" set + constitutional documents + diverse system prompts/tool defs) achieves 0% blackmail rate for Claude Haiku 4.5 onward vs. up to 96% for Opus 4; 3M tokens of Difficult Advice match 85M tokens of synthetic honeypot data (28× efficiency); constitutional documents alone reduce blackmail 65% → 19% | source: https://www.anthropic.com/research/teaching-claude-why | status: open ; relates-to [anthropic-research-2026-05-07-natural-language-autoencoders] — NLAs measure the eval-awareness gap; this paper closes it from the training side
- **2026-05-09** [arxiv:2605.06651] CLAIM: DeepMind "AI Co-Mathematician" workbench (asynchronous stateful workspace with hypothesis tracking and uncertainty management) reaches 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4 — claimed new high among AI systems; helped researchers solve open problems in early human testing | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06651 | status: open
- **2026-05-09** [arxiv:2605.06642] CLAIM: StraTA (top-down strategy abstraction sampled from initial task state, conditions all subsequent actions, jointly trained via hierarchical GRPO with diverse strategy rollouts and self-judgment) reaches ALFWorld 93.1%, WebShop 84.2%, SciWorld 63.5% — claimed to outperform frontier closed-source models on SciWorld | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06642 | status: open ; near-tie with [arxiv:2605.06078] BEACON 92.9% ALFWorld via mechanistically distinct mechanism (top-down strategy abstraction vs. bottom-up milestone reward shaping) — open question whether these compose

- **2026-05-09** [arxiv:2605.06285] CLAIM: LatentRAG (latent thoughts/subqueries emitted directly from hidden states in single forward pass, LLM aligned with dense retriever in shared latent space, parallel decoder for transparency) reduces inference latency ~90% vs. explicit agentic RAG with comparable accuracy across 7 benchmarks | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06285 | status: open ; awaits independent reproduction with strongest text-RAG baselines on equivalent compute
- **2026-05-09** [arxiv:2605.06455] CLAIM: PrefixGuard (StepView trace adapter + supervised prefix-risk monitor) reaches AUPRC 0.900 WebArena / 0.710 τ²-Bench / 0.533 SkillsBench / 0.557 TerminalBench; +0.137 AUPRC mean over raw-text baselines; LLM-as-judge underperforms supervised monitors significantly under same protocol | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06455 | status: open ; trajectory-level analogue to [arxiv:2605.04785] AgentTrust (call-level interception)
- **2026-05-09** [arxiv:2605.06638] CLAIM: ScaleLogic controlled framework shows RL training compute follows power law T ∝ D^γ in reasoning depth (R² > 0.99); exponent γ rises monotonically from 1.04 to 2.60 as logical expressiveness increases (negation, quantification, etc.); training on more expressive structures yields up to +10.66 points downstream transfer | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06638 | status: open ; methodological constraint on [arxiv:2605.00425] AEM, [arxiv:2605.06078] BEACON, [arxiv:2605.06642] StraTA — credit assignment improvements bounded by training-data expressiveness
- **2026-05-09** [hf-blog-2026-05-08-allenai-emo] CLAIM: Allen AI EMO — 1B-active / 14B-total MoE (8 active of 128 experts, 1T tokens) — uses document-level expert pooling + global load balancing + variable pool sizing during training; serves at 12.5% of experts (16/128) with ~3% accuracy drop, 25% with ~1% drop; experts cluster on semantic domains (Health, Politics, Film/Music) rather than surface features; standard MoEs collapse to near-random in same selective regime | source: https://huggingface.co/blog/allenai/emo | status: open ; weights, code, visualization released publicly
- **2026-05-10** [arxiv:2604.15597] CLAIM: DELEGATE-52 (long delegated document-editing benchmark, 52 professional domains) — Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude 4.6 Opus, GPT-5.4 corrupt an average of 25% of document content by end of long workflows; errors are sparse but severe; agentic tool use does NOT improve performance; document size, interaction length, and distractor files all worsen results | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15597 | status: open ; sharpens [arxiv:2605.00136] (tool-use tax) and [arxiv:2605.06635] (deep-research factual-accuracy decay with tool depth) — three independent confirmations that frontier-model agent reliability degrades non-trivially with interaction length and tools don't paper over it
- **2026-05-10** [arxiv:2605.05413] CLAIM: Constant-Context Skill Learning (lightweight task-family modules conditioning only on current observation + compact state block; procedural context moved from prompts into weights) reaches 89.6% ALFWorld unseen / 76.8% WebShop / 66.4% SciWorld unseen with Qwen3-8B (SFT+RL); 2-7× per-turn prompt-token reduction vs. controlled ReAct baselines | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413 | status: open ; relates-to [arxiv:2605.06078] BEACON (92.9% ALFWorld) and [arxiv:2605.06642] StraTA (93.1% ALFWorld) — three mechanically distinct mechanisms converging at the ALFWorld near-90% plateau (in-weights procedural memory vs. milestone reward shaping vs. top-down strategy abstraction); also relates-to [arxiv:2605.04050] LCM (both attack the "context fills up" failure)
- **2026-05-10** [arxiv:2605.05716] CLAIM: full-factorial study of all 32 combinations of {planning, tools, memory, self-reflection, retrieval} across HotpotQA + GSM8K, Llama-3.1 8B + 70B, up to 10 seeds (96 conditions) — single-tool agent beats All-In on HotpotQA by 32% F1 (0.233 vs 0.177, p=0.023); 3-component subset beats full system on GSM8K by 79%; 56.3% submodularity violations across configurations; optimal subsets are task- and model-scale-dependent | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05716 | status: open ; methodologically supports [arxiv:2605.00136] tool-use tax and [arxiv:2604.15597] DELEGATE-52 from the scaffolding angle
- **2026-05-10** [arxiv:2605.05245] CLAIM: AdaGATE (training-free entity-centric gap tracking + targeted micro-query generation + utility-based selection for multi-hop RAG) reaches Evidence F1 62.3% clean HotpotQA / 71.2% under redundancy injection; uses 2.6× fewer input tokens than Adaptive-k baseline | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05245 | status: open ; complementary-to [arxiv:2605.05242] DCI (DCI replaces coarse retrieval; AdaGATE optimizes the agent's evidence-assembly loop)
- **2026-05-10** [arxiv:2605.04036] CLAIM: OpenSeeker-v2 (SFT-only on 10.6k high-quality trajectories, expanded knowledge graph + broader tools + strict low-step filtering) — 30B model reaches BrowseComp 46.0% / BrowseComp-ZH 58.1% / Humanity's Last Exam 34.6% / xbench 78.0%, beating Tongyi DeepResearch (43.4 / 46.7 / 32.9 / 75.0); claimed first SOTA search agent in class trained purely via SFT by an academic team; weights to be released | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04036 | status: open ; sits below [arxiv:2605.05191] LongSeeker (BrowseComp 61.5) — suggests inference-time context-management paradigm > SFT trajectory scale within open-30B class
- **2026-05-10** [arxiv:2605.05583] CLAIM: Belief Memory (each entry retains multiple candidate conclusions with associated probabilities, updated via Noisy-OR rules; retrieval surfaces alternatives with probability scores) achieves best average performance vs. baselines on LoCoMo + ALFWorld even with limited training data | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05583 | status: open ; directly attacks failure mode in [arxiv:2605.06527] STALE (55.2% cap on implicit-conflict detection) by never committing to a single conclusion in the first place; open empirical question whether BeliefMem moves the STALE number — authors did not test on STALE per the abstract
- **2026-05-10** [arxiv:2605.03042] CLAIM: ARIS (open-source autonomous research framework, 3 layers — Execution: 65+ Markdown skills + MCP + persistent research wiki + deterministic figure generation; Orchestration: 5 end-to-end workflows + adjustable effort + configurable reviewer routing; Assurance: integrity verification, result-to-claim mapping, claim auditing, 5-pass scientific editing pipeline, math-proof checks, visual PDF inspection) defaults to cross-model adversarial collaboration (executor model + reviewer from different model family); names central failure mode "plausible unsupported success — long-running agent produces claims whose evidential support is incomplete, misreported, or silently inherited" | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03042 | status: open ; evolution of [arxiv:2605.06651] AI Co-Mathematician's stateful workspace generalized beyond math; Markdown-skill convention converges with [arxiv:2605.06614] SkillOS
- **2026-05-10** [anthropic-news-2026-05-05-finance-agents] CLAIM: Anthropic ships 10 financial-services agent templates (Claude Cowork/Code plugins + Managed Agents) decomposed as Skills + Connectors + Subagents; powered by Claude Opus 4.7 scoring 64.37% on Vals AI Finance Agent benchmark (claimed industry-leading); 8 new connectors + Moody's MCP app with access to >600M public/private companies | source: https://www.anthropic.com/news/finance-agents | status: open ; vendor-self-reported on third-party Vals AI benchmark; first large third-party data-provider MCP at this scale shipped through App Marketplace flow
- **2026-05-11** [arxiv:2605.08060] CLAIM: "Memory Curse" — expanded accessible history degrades cooperation in 18 of 28 model-game settings across 7 LLMs, 4 cooperation games, 500+ rounds (378K reasoning traces analyzed); mechanism via lexical traces + LoRA cognitive probes + memory sanitization: longer recall erodes forward-looking cooperative *intent* (not distrust); explicit deliberation *amplifies* the curse | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08060 | status: open ; contradicts the implicit "more memory is better" assumption in [arxiv:2605.06614] SkillOS, [arxiv:2605.05413] Constant-Context Skill Learning, [arxiv:2605.05583] Belief Memory; open question whether structured memory (skills/beliefs) inherits the curse
- **2026-05-11** [arxiv:2605.07313] CLAIM: Scale-Conditioned Agent Memory Eval (holds task evidence fixed while adding irrelevant sessions) — HippoRAG reliability falls 16–20 pp as irrelevant sessions accumulate even within budget; Qwen3-8B exceeds memory-call budget at scale; Qwen3-32B/235B remain reliable in tested range; 4 diagnostic metrics (budget-compliant reliability, memory-call burden, failure category, usability boundary) across LongMemEval + LoCoMo × flat/planar/hierarchical interfaces | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07313 | status: open ; sharpens [arxiv:2605.06527] STALE and [arxiv:2605.05583] Belief Memory by adding third orthogonal failure mode (scale-induced usability loss)
- **2026-05-11** [arxiv:2605.07242] CLAIM: MEMOREPAIR (three-stage barrier-first cascade repair: Withdrawal → Construction → Publication; publication problem reduces to maximum-weight predecessor closure / s-t min-cut) reduces invalidated-memory exposure 69.8–94.3% → 0%, recovers 91.1–94.3% of validated successors vs. exhaustive repair, normalized repair cost 1.00 → 0.57–0.76; tested on ToolBench + MemoryArena | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07242 | status: open ; provides implementation layer for derivative-artifact consistency that Belief Memory and SkillOS hand-wave over
- **2026-05-11** [arxiv:2605.07990] CLAIM: tool-call selection is a linear feature — mean-difference activation vectors switch tool choice with 77–100% accuracy single-turn (93–100% on 4B+); the row of the output layer producing the target tool's first token concentrates the effect, a single unit vector along that row achieves 93–100% steering; gap between top-1 and top-2 tool logits predicts wrong calls (Gemma 3 12B/27B: narrow-gap calls produce 14–21× more wrong calls); base models encode tool identity at 69–82% cosine-readout despite 2–10% generation accuracy; tested on Gemma 3, Qwen 3, Qwen 2.5, Llama 3.1, 270M–27B | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07990 | status: open ; gives mechanistic detail to [arxiv:2605.00136] tool-use tax — the tax isn't in tool *choice*
- **2026-05-11** [arxiv:2605.07112] CLAIM: Switchcraft (DistilBERT classifier routing function-calling queries across backend models) reports 82.9% accuracy matching or exceeding the best individual model at 84% lower inference cost, saving $3,600+ per million queries on 5 function-calling benchmarks; bigger models aren't strictly better at tool-use; "cheap" reasoning models can become expensive by burning extra tokens on tool-call deliberation | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07112 | status: open ; production-pattern paper for MCP gateways; pairs with [arxiv:2605.07990] (logit-gap = runtime confidence signal; Switchcraft = pre-call router)
- **2026-05-11** [arxiv:2605.07161] CLAIM: SREGym — live SRE benchmark on real cloud-native stacks with fault injectors producing metastable + correlated failures + ambient noise across architectural layers; 90 realistic problems; frontier agents show up to 40% end-to-end performance variation across failure categories; open source and actively maintained | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07161 | status: open ; correct methodological response to ranking-instability problems in static-trace benchmarks like [arxiv:2605.04624] AuditRepairBench; 40% spread is eval-result analogue of [arxiv:2605.05716] cross-component-interference finding
- **2026-05-11** [arxiv:2605.08083] CLAIM: AutoTTS (Google) reframes test-time scaling as controller synthesis over pre-collected reasoning trajectories — controllers choose branch/continue/probe/prune/stop actions evaluated against cached trajectories rather than fresh LLM calls; reports better accuracy-cost tradeoffs than hand-tuned TTS baselines and generalization to held-out benchmarks and model scales; discovery cost $39.9 and 160 minutes per strategy | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08083 | status: open ; generalizable pattern beyond TTS to subagent dispatch and tool-call sequencing
- **2026-05-11** [anthropic-research-2026-05-07-donating-open-source-petri] CLAIM: Anthropic transfers Petri 3.0 (open-source agentic-alignment auditing suite — auditor + judge models running scripted alignment scenarios incl. deception, sycophancy, harmful compliance) to Meridian Labs (independent AI eval nonprofit); Petri 3.0 adds adaptable architecture, Dish integration for real-system-prompt realism, Bloom integration for deeper assessment; Petri used in every Claude release since Sonnet 4.5 and by UK AISI for sabotage-risk evals; sits alongside Inspect + Scout in Meridian's open evaluation stack | source: https://www.anthropic.com/research/donating-open-source-petri | status: open ; third leg with [anthropic-research-2026-05-07-natural-language-autoencoders] (measure eval-awareness gap) and [anthropic-research-2026-05-08-teaching-claude-why] (close it from training side) — Petri is the measurement infrastructure
- **2026-05-12** [arxiv:2605.10870] CLAIM: DeMem reframes agent memory as a decision-centric rate-distortion problem; derives exact forgetting boundary + memory-distortion frontier; online learner refines memory partition only when data certify decision-conflict at a shared state; near-minimax regret guarantees; consistent gains under matched runtime budgets on synthetic diagnostics + long-horizon conversational benchmarks | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10870 | status: open ; adds 6th orthogonal axis to memory-failure-mode taxonomy alongside [arxiv:2605.06527] STALE, [arxiv:2605.05583] Belief Memory, [arxiv:2605.07313] scale-conditioned, [arxiv:2605.07242] MEMOREPAIR, [arxiv:2605.08060] Memory Curse — open question whether decision-distortion is unifying lens or independent failure mode
- **2026-05-12** [arxiv:2605.10913] CLAIM: Shepherd (Stanford et al.) provides Lean-mechanized functional programming model for meta-agent operations with typed event execution trace; process/filesystem forking 5× faster than Docker with >95% prompt-cache reuse on replay; runtime intervention boosts pair-coding pass rate 28.8% → 54.7%; counterfactual meta-optimization +11 pp; Tree-RL training 34.2% → 39.4%; open-sourced | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10913 | status: open ; runtime substrate that LCM [arxiv:2605.04050], SkillOS [arxiv:2605.06614], AutoTTS [arxiv:2605.08083], LongSeeker [arxiv:2605.05191] could all compose on top of
- **2026-05-12** [arxiv:2605.10787] CLAIM: ComplexMCP — first MCP-native benchmark at scale (300+ tools across 7 stateful sandboxes); top-tier frontier models <60% success vs. ~90% human; three named failure modes: (1) tool retrieval degrades as action space expands, (2) overconfidence in agent decisions, (3) abandonment over recovery | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10787 | status: open ; extends [arxiv:2605.00334] AgentFloor short-horizon ceiling along scale + statefulness axes; complements [arxiv:2605.06334] MANTRA as the two production-realistic tool-use benchmarks
- **2026-05-12** [microsoft-research-2026-05-11-socialreasoning-bench] CLAIM: Microsoft SocialReasoning-Bench (calendar coordination + marketplace negotiation domains) introduces Outcome Optimality + Due Diligence metrics; GPT-4.1, GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3 Flash all hit near-perfect task completion but ≈0 marketplace Outcome Optimality (accept deals giving away virtually all surplus); defensive prompting yields GPT-5.4 +0.21 calendar / +0.12 marketplace; adversarial counterparties further degrade Outcome Optimality and agents rarely refuse | source: https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/research/blog/socialreasoning-bench-measuring-whether-ai-agents-act-in-users-best-interests/ | status: open ; capability-side analogue of [anthropic-research-2026-05-07-natural-language-autoencoders] eval-awareness gap and [arxiv:2604.15597] DELEGATE-52 long-workflow corruption — "completion ≠ value" failure mode
- **2026-05-12** [arxiv:2605.10828] CLAIM: "First Drop of Ink" — long-context reasoning degrades sharply at small proportions of misleading documents with diminishing additional impact as proportion grows (concave damage function); attention analysis shows hard distractors capture disproportionate attention regardless of quantity; recovery requires near-elimination of misleading info, not dilution | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10828 | status: open ; reinforces [arxiv:2605.05716] "More Is Not Always Better" cross-component interference and [arxiv:2605.05245] AdaGATE — three independent findings that retrieval precision dominates k
- **2026-05-12** [arxiv:2605.09649] CLAIM: Learnable KV cache eviction via retention gates that assign utility scores; tokens compete for cache capacity across layers and heads under unified policy; trimmed cache matches or surpasses full-cache inference (not just approximates) across language, vision-language, dialogue tasks (Yale) | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09649 | status: open ; complements [arxiv:2605.04050] LCM — LCM compresses content; KV-cache eviction compresses attention substrate; direct relevance to M5 Max long-context inference where KV cache (not weights) is binding constraint
- **2026-05-12** [arxiv:2605.10448] CLAIM: Evidence-Supported Bounds — outcome-evidence reporting layer added on top of existing agent benchmarks (no task/agent modification); each run labeled Evidence Pass / Evidence Fail / Unknown; aggregate scores reported as bounds; tested on ANDROIDWORLD, AGENTDOJO, APPWORLD, τ³-bench retail, MINIWOB; separates uncertain cases from aggregate success | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10448 | status: open ; third leg with [arxiv:2605.04624] AuditRepairBench (ranking instability) and [arxiv:2605.07161] SREGym (live vs. static traces) on agent benchmark credibility
- **2026-05-12** [arxiv:2605.10805] CLAIM: RACER (ICML 2026) — reasoning judges substantially improve accuracy on structured-verification tasks (math, code) but offer limited/negative gains on simpler evals at significantly higher compute cost; dynamic router between reasoning and non-reasoning judges as constrained distributionally robust optimization with KL-divergence uncertainty set; primal-dual algorithm with proven uniqueness + linear convergence | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10805 | status: open ; pairs with [arxiv:2605.07112] Switchcraft as the two routing axes — Switchcraft routes workers; RACER routes judges
- **2026-05-13** [arxiv:2605.12477] CLAIM: MEME (multi-entity & evolving memory eval, 6 tasks, 100 controlled episodes × 6 memory systems × 3 paradigms) — all evaluated systems collapse on dependency-reasoning under default configuration: **Cascade 3% / Absence 1% average accuracy**; prompt optimization, deeper retrieval, reduced noise, stronger LLMs all fail to substantially improve; only file-based agent on Claude Opus 4.7 partially closes the gap at **~70× baseline cost** | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12477 | status: open ; 7th orthogonal memory failure mode alongside [arxiv:2605.06527] STALE, [arxiv:2605.05583] Belief Memory, [arxiv:2605.07313] scale-conditioned, [arxiv:2605.07242] MEMOREPAIR, [arxiv:2605.08060] Memory Curse, [arxiv:2605.10870] DeMem — first benchmark to specifically target dependency/absence/deletion recall; open empirical question whether Belief Memory and MEMOREPAIR move the MEME numbers
- **2026-05-13** [arxiv:2605.12493] CLAIM: LongMemEval-V2 extends LongMemEval to 451 manually-curated questions over histories up to 500 trajectories / 115M tokens, 5 memory abilities (static state recall, dynamic state tracking, workflow knowledge, environment-specific gotchas, premise awareness); AgentRunbook-C (coding-agent-based, builds executable runbook) reaches 72.5% average vs. AgentRunbook-R (RAG) 48.5% and unstructured coding agent baseline 69.3% | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12493 | status: open ; converges with MEME and SkillOS on "managed file system as memory" design point
- **2026-05-13** [arxiv:2605.12357] CLAIM: δ-mem augments a frozen full-attention LLM backbone with a compact **8×8 online associative memory state** producing low-rank attention corrections at generation time; no weight modification; reports 1.10× strongest non-δ-mem baseline overall, **1.31× MemoryAgentBench, 1.20× LoCoMo**; backbone capabilities preserved | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12357 | status: open ; mechanistically orthogonal to [arxiv:2605.09649] KV cache eviction — δ-mem compresses episodic memory, KV eviction compresses attention substrate; awaits weight/code release for independent reproduction
- **2026-05-13** [arxiv:2605.09998] CLAIM: Continual Harness (reset-free self-improving harness, iteratively refines prompts + sub-agents + skills + memory) — GPP instance becomes **first AI to complete Pokemon Blue, Yellow Legacy hard mode, and Crystal without a lost battle** from minimal environment interface (no curated knowledge, no hand-crafted tools, no domain scaffolding); on Pokemon Red/Emerald substantially reduces button-press cost vs. minimalist baseline and recovers majority of gap to hand-engineered expert harness | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09998 | status: open ; concrete proof point for long-running autonomous loops; embodied/world-model analogue of [arxiv:2605.06614] SkillOS and [arxiv:2605.03042] ARIS
- **2026-05-13** [arxiv:2605.12481] CLAIM: ToolCUA (3-stage training: Interleaved GUI-Tool Trajectory Scaling → Tool-Bootstrapped GUI RFT → Online Agentic RL with Tool-Efficient Path Reward) reaches **46.85% on OSWorld-MCP, +66% relative over baseline, +3.9% over GUI-only configurations**, claimed SOTA at comparable scale | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12481 | status: open ; extends [arxiv:2605.10787] ComplexMCP MCP-at-scale ceiling; pairs with [arxiv:2605.07112] Switchcraft as two routing approaches (in-model vs. cross-model dispatch)
- **2026-05-13** [arxiv:2605.11946] CLAIM: Counterfactual Trace Auditing pairs with-skill vs. without-skill traces on 49 Claude SWE tasks, segments + aligns + emits Skill Influence Pattern (SIP) annotations; pass rate moves only **+0.3 pp on average** but **522 SIP instances** appear across paired traces (literal template copying, off-task artifact creation, excess planning, task recovery); high-baseline tasks show most skill effects but are pass-rate-saturated; mid-baseline tasks show most recoverable improvement at substantially higher compute cost; code released | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11946 | status: open ; sharpens [arxiv:2605.06614] SkillOS by exposing that aggregate gains hide mixed-sign behavior changes; methodologically related to [arxiv:2605.10448] Evidence-Supported Bounds as a trace-level reporting layer
- **2026-05-13** [arxiv:2605.12131] CLAIM: Rollout Cards — audit of 50 popular agent training/eval repositories finds **none report failure / error / skip counts** alongside headline scores; 37 distinct reporting rules alter task-success rate, token accounting, or timing on identical evidence; re-grading the same rollouts under different rules produces score swings up to **20.9 absolute percentage points**, sometimes flipping model rankings; reference implementation in open-source Ergon RL gym | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12131 | status: open ; third leg with [arxiv:2605.10448] Evidence-Supported Bounds and [arxiv:2605.04624] AuditRepairBench on agent-benchmark credibility — three independent papers converging on "rollout records, not scores, are the reproducibility unit"
- **2026-05-13** [arxiv:2605.12087] CLAIM: Position paper proposes typed, structured, addressable, versioned, dependency-aware, authoritative intermediate artifacts as a data model for agentic systems — distinct from chat transcripts, memory, hidden reasoning state, narration; explicit update semantics and lineage tracking | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12087 | status: open ; converges with [arxiv:2605.10913] Shepherd typed-event execution trace and [arxiv:2605.06614] SkillOS Markdown SkillRepo on "external agent state needs to be a typed, addressable, versioned data structure, not a log"
- **2026-05-14** [arxiv:2605.12978] CLAIM: GPT-5.4 fails on **54% of ARC-AGI problems it had previously solved without memory** once the same LLM consolidates wins into a textual memory bank; memory utility first improves then degrades below no-memory baseline; consolidation identified as the core failure source; recommended fix = retain raw episode traces as primary evidence and gate consolidation explicitly | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12978 | status: open ; **8th orthogonal memory failure mode** alongside [arxiv:2605.06527] STALE, [arxiv:2605.05583] Belief Memory, [arxiv:2605.07313] scale-conditioned, [arxiv:2605.07242] MEMOREPAIR, [arxiv:2605.08060] Memory Curse, [arxiv:2605.10870] DeMem, [arxiv:2605.12477] MEME — "consolidation-decay"; **conflicts with [arxiv:2605.06614] SkillOS** which uses LLM-driven Markdown SkillRepo consolidation as its core mechanism; open empirical question whether SkillOS retains gains when curator/executor share backbone or whether item 1's failure reverses when a different model performs consolidation
- **2026-05-14** [arxiv:2605.12922] CLAIM: "Channel-transition" mechanistic account of multi-turn LLM decay — task-defining tokens become inaccessible to attention while still encoded in residual stream; Goal Accessibility Ratio (GAR) metric introduced; Mistral 20-fact recall collapses **near-perfect → 11%** under forced attention closure; linear probes on residuals predict outcome at **up to 0.99 AUC**; goal-info layer depth varies 2–27 across architectures | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12922 | status: open ; mechanistic complement to [arxiv:2605.10828] First Drop of Ink (attention concentration on distractors); operational implication = periodic goal re-anchoring > longer context for multi-turn agents
- **2026-05-14** [arxiv:2605.13050] CLAIM: DeepMind-affiliated "Context Training with Active Information Seeking" — context optimizer equipped with Wikipedia search + browser tools; naive integration degrades performance, but pairing tools with search-based training procedure that maintains and prunes multiple candidate contexts yields gains across Flores+, HealthBench, LiveCodeBench, Humanity's Last Exam; optimized contexts claim cross-model generalization | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13050 | status: open ; relates-to [arxiv:2605.05191] LongSeeker (Context-ReAct operators) and [arxiv:2605.04050] LCM (recursive context compression) on "context optimization is itself an agent loop with tools, not a passive shrinking step"
- **2026-05-14** [arxiv:2605.13037] CLAIM: MAP (Map-then-Act paradigm — Global Exploration → Task-Specific Mapping → Knowledge-Augmented Execution; plug-and-play) enables frontier models to **surpass near-zero baseline on 22 of 25 ARC-AGI-3 game environments**; releases MAP-2K paired dataset; argues understanding the environment before action is more fundamental than reactive planning or imitation | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13037 | status: open ; second ARC-AGI-3 success in a week via different mechanism from [arxiv:2605.05138] Executable World Models (7/25 fully solved via Python world models) — open empirical question whether the two compose
- **2026-05-14** [arxiv:2605.12673] CLAIM: BenchJack (Berkeley) — automated red-teaming pipeline against agent benchmarks finds **219 distinct reward-hacking flaws across 8 classes** in 10 popular benchmarks; "hackable-task ratio" drops from ~100% to <10% after iterative patching on 4 benchmarks; **WebArena and OSWorld fully patched within 3 iterations**; releases Agent-Eval Checklist | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12673 | status: open ; 5th paper in the agent-eval credibility thread joining [arxiv:2605.04624] AuditRepairBench, [arxiv:2605.10448] Evidence-Supported Bounds, [arxiv:2605.12131] Rollout Cards, [arxiv:2605.11946] Counterfactual Trace Auditing — BenchJack attacks the task definition itself
- **2026-05-14** [arxiv:2605.12925] CLAIM: AgentLens — analysis of 2,614 trajectories from 8 models on 60 SWE-bench Verified tasks finds **10.7% of passing solutions are "Lucky Passes"** (regression cycles, blind retries, missing verification, temporally disordered exploration); per-model Lucky rate ranges **0.5% → 23.2%**; releases 1,815 trajectories labeled Lucky/Solid/Ideal; **up to 5-position model rank shifts** when ranked by process quality instead of pass rate | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12925 | status: open ; SWE-bench-specific instantiation of [arxiv:2605.11946] Counterfactual Trace Auditing's "aggregate gains hide mixed-sign behavior changes" finding
- **2026-05-14** [arxiv:2605.12975] CLAIM: PyRAG reformulates multi-hop RAG as program synthesis and execution — Python programs call retrieval as a tool, get deterministic feedback, self-repair against compiler/runtime errors; consistent gains over strong baselines on PopQA, HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, MuSiQue, Bamboogle under both training-free and RL-trained settings | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12975 | status: open ; multi-hop-specific complement to [arxiv:2605.05242] Direct Corpus Interaction (which replaced general retrieval with code-over-corpus); training-free result the operationally useful one for existing MCP-based stacks
- **2026-05-14** [arxiv:2605.13301] CLAIM: SU-01 (30B-A3B reasoning backbone, ~340K curriculum-SFT trajectories + ~200 RL steps + test-time scaling, reasoning trajectories beyond 100K tokens) claims gold-medal-level performance on IMO 2025 / USAMO 2026 / IPhO 2024 / IPhO 2025; open recipe | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13301 | status: open ; same parameter class as Qwen3.6-35B-A3B → local-fit math reasoning subagent on M5 Max once weights release; recipe overlaps with [arxiv:2605.06326] Teaching Thinking Models to Reason with Tools (TIR)
- **2026-05-16** [arxiv:2605.15184] CLAIM: controlled head-to-head — grep generally outperforms vector retrieval as agent memory backend across 116 LongMemEval questions and four production harnesses (Chronos, Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI); harness choice dominates retrieval method on absolute score with identical data; robustness probed by progressively injecting distracting conversation history | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15184 | status: open ; quantifies [arxiv:2605.05242] Direct Corpus Interaction qualitative claim; pairs with [arxiv:2605.12493] LongMemEval-V2 AgentRunbook-C and [arxiv:2605.12477] MEME file-based-on-Opus-4.7 — three independent papers point at file system + keyword as production-default memory substrate
- **2026-05-16** [arxiv:2605.14498] CLAIM: GroupMemBench (multi-party 3+ speaker conversations, six query categories: multi-hop, knowledge updates, term ambiguity, temporal, +2) — strongest agent memory system caps at **46.0% average accuracy** (knowledge-update 27.1%, term-ambiguity 37.7%); **simple BM25 baseline matches or beats most agent memory systems**; structural and lexical features of multi-party conversation not preserved by current memory pipelines | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14498 | status: open ; **9th orthogonal memory failure mode** (multi-party belief tracking) alongside STALE / Belief Memory / scale-conditioned / MEMOREPAIR / Memory Curse / DeMem / MEME / consolidation-decay; BM25-matches finding reinforces [arxiv:2605.15184]
- **2026-05-16** [arxiv:2605.13941] CLAIM: EvolveMem (LLM-powered diagnosis module reads retrieval-failure logs, proposes memory-architecture configuration changes with automatic safeguards; co-evolves stored knowledge and retrieval mechanism) reaches **+25.7% relative over strongest baseline on LoCoMo (+78% vs. minimal)**, **+18.9% over strongest on MemBench**; configurations transfer across benchmarks suggesting generalizable retrieval principles; code released as SimpleMem | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941 | status: open ; operationally compatible with [arxiv:2605.12978] consolidation-decay finding because evolution happens on *architecture* not *content*; open question whether the diagnosis-loop avoids the consolidation-decay trap on ARC-AGI-Stream

- **2026-05-17** [microsoft-research-2026-05-15-delegation-followup] CLAIM: MSR follow-up to [arxiv:2604.15597] DELEGATE-52 (Philippe Laban, Tobias Schnabel, Jennifer Neville) re-affirms **19–34% artifact-fidelity degradation over 20 delegated iterations** for frontier models, adds new contrast that **Python workflows degrade <1% on average** under the same protocol; explicitly frames original paper as diagnostic stress test (not comprehensive reliability measure) and endorses verification loops + orchestration + domain-specific tooling as production mitigation | source: https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/research/blog/further-notes-on-our-recent-research-on-ai-delegation-and-long-horizon-reliability/ | status: confirms-and-extends [arxiv:2604.15597] DELEGATE-52 ; Python-as-durable-state aligns with [arxiv:2605.12975] PyRAG and [arxiv:2605.05138] Executable World Models

- **2026-05-17** [arxiv:2605.15155] CLAIM: SDAR (Zhejiang U cluster — Yueting Zhuang) combines RL with on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) via sigmoid-gated auxiliary objective that strengthens positive signals and softly attenuates negative ones, fixing compounding instability of naive token-level distillation in multi-turn LLM agents; **ALFWorld +9.4% / WebShop accuracy +10.2% / Search-QA +7.0% over GRPO baseline** across Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 model families | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15155 | status: open ; joins the ALFWorld near-90% plateau cluster with [arxiv:2605.06078] BEACON 92.9%, [arxiv:2605.06642] StraTA 93.1%, [arxiv:2605.05413] Constant-Context Skill Learning 89.6% — four mechanistically distinct techniques converging at the plateau

- **2026-05-21** [arxiv:2605.19660] CLAIM: OScaR (HKU — Zunhai Su et al.) extreme KV cache quantization; identifies **Token Norm Imbalance** as primary bottleneck to per-channel quant fidelity; uses per-channel canalized rotation + omni-token scaling; **near-lossless INT2 across text / multi-modal / omni-modal LLMs**; **3.0× decode speedup, 5.3× memory reduction, 4.1× throughput vs. BF16 FlashDecoding-v2**; code public at github.com/ZunhaiSu/OScaR-KV-Quant | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19660 | status: open ; composes orthogonally with [arxiv:2605.18643] ZEDA (active-expert reduction) — KV side + expert side on the same memory budget; local-fit headline for M5 Max long-context inference

- **2026-05-21** [arxiv:2605.20315] CLAIM: Mix-Quant (NUS — Xinchao Wang cluster) phase-aware quantization for agentic LLMs; quantizing whole inference pipeline degrades accuracy, but **prefill stage shows substantial quantization redundancy and can be FP4-quantized with minimal accuracy loss** while preserving BF16 for decoding; **up to 3× prefill speedup** across long-context and agentic benchmarks | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20315 | status: open ; argues production-realistic agentic inference is **asymmetric quant** (prefill ≠ decode); composes with OScaR (KV side) and ZEDA (active expert side) as three orthogonal optimizations on the same model + hardware budget

- **2026-05-21** [arxiv:2605.21468] CLAIM: RELEX (Yu Meng UVA cluster) — RLVR weight-update trajectories are **extremely low-rank**; rank-1 approximation captures majority of downstream gains; estimating rank-1 subspace from short observation window enables linear-regression extrapolation; matches full RLVR performance with **as few as 15% of training steps**, extrapolation **10–20× beyond observed prefix**; mechanism attributed to denoising effect from subspace projection | source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21468 | status: open ; third independent low-rank linearity finding in the agentic LLM stack alongside [arxiv:2605.07990] (tool calling linearly readable in logits) and [arxiv:2605.14038] (tool-necessity orthogonal probe directions) — three different surfaces converging on dominant linear structure
